- **AGENTS-akhil**: Load this **in addition to** the root `AGENTS.md` when the person asking is Akhil. Do not apply `AGENTS-prakrit.md`. ([PrakritR/PropLane](https://github.com/PrakritR/PropLane/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/AGENTS-akhil.md))
- **agents-and-skills-architecture**: 根目录 `AGENTS.md` 给出协作和安全边界，目录内 `AGENTS.md` 增加更具体约束。`.agents/skills/` 是仓库 Skills 唯一的 authoritative source。 ([openapi-to/openapi-to](https://github.com/openapi-to/openapi-to/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/agents-and-skills-architecture.md) · ★1)
- **agents-architect-agent-author**: Writes sub-agent definition files (agents/*.md) for a new plugin given a DOMAIN.md and an agent plan. Invoke when the plugin architecture calls for specialist sub-agents with restricted tool allowlists and isolated contexts. ([Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect](https://github.com/Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect/blob/HEAD/agents/arch-agent-author.md) · ★1)
- **agents-architect-command-author**: Writes slash-command files (commands/&lt;ns&gt;/&lt;cmd&gt;.md) for a new plugin given a COMMAND_PLAN. Invoke when the plugin needs user-facing /commands that orchestrate skills and sub-agents into repeatable workflows. ([Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect](https://github.com/Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect/blob/HEAD/agents/arch-command-author.md) · ★1)
- **agents-architect-context-architect**: Designs the context management layer for a new plugin — CLAUDE.md, memory tiers, scratchpad schemas, read-depth rules, compaction strategy. Invoke after skills/agents/commands are planned so the context layer reflects the full workflow graph. ([Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect](https://github.com/Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect/blob/HEAD/agents/arch-context-architect.md) · ★1)
- **agents-architect-domain-researcher**: Researches a domain end-to-end and produces DOMAIN.md for a new plugin build. Invoke when the user runs /agents-architect:new &lt;domain&gt; or /agents-architect:research &lt;domain&gt;, when the orchestrator needs a cited survey of tools/MCPs/practices/anti-patterns before scaffolding skills. ([Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect](https://github.com/Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect/blob/HEAD/agents/arch-domain-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **agents-architect-evaluator**: Runs the eval harness against a newly generated plugin — skill trigger accuracy, agent delegation correctness, command workflow integrity, regression suite. Invoke after /agents-architect:new packaging and whenever a plugin is iterated, to score and report. ([Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect](https://github.com/Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect/blob/HEAD/agents/arch-evaluator.md) · ★1)
- **agents-architect-mcp-scout**: Searches the MCP registry and prior art to identify MCP servers for a given domain, producing MCP_PLAN.md with recommendations and fallbacks. Invoke during /agents-architect:new right after domain research. ([Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect](https://github.com/Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect/blob/HEAD/agents/arch-mcp-scout.md) · ★1)
- **agents-architect-plugin-packager**: Packages a newly scaffolded set of skills/agents/commands/hooks/MCPs into a valid, installable Claude Code plugin with manifest, marketplace entry, README, CHANGELOG, and LICENSE. Invoke as the final step of /agents-architect:new after all authoring agents finish. ([Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect](https://github.com/Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect/blob/HEAD/agents/arch-plugin-packager.md) · ★1)
- **agents-architect-skill-author**: Writes SKILL.md files for a new plugin given a DOMAIN.md and a skill plan. Invoke after domain research and architecture planning, when the orchestrator needs one-to-many skill files authored with correct trigger-phrase engineering and progressive-disclosure body structure. ([Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect](https://github.com/Ibrahim-3d/agents-architect/blob/HEAD/agents/arch-skill-author.md) · ★1)
- **AGENTS-ARCHITECTURE**: The *shape* of the engine: where things live, what owns what, how the pieces fit. Conventions: [`CLAUDE-BASELINE.md`](CLAUDE-BASELINE.md) and [`.claude/rules/`](../../.claude/rules/); build: [`BUILD.md`](BUILD.md); fleet workflow: [`FLEET.md`](FLEET.md). ([jakildev/IrredenEngine](https://github.com/jakildev/IrredenEngine/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/AGENTS-ARCHITECTURE.md) · ★10)
- **agents-audit**: Audit agent infrastructure — token overhead, rule triggers, AGENTS.md health, Capability-over-Structure adherence, stale references. Read-only, suggest-only, never auto-apply. ([event4u-app/agent-config](https://github.com/event4u-app/agent-config/blob/HEAD/dist/agent-src/commands/agents/audit.md) · ★19)
- **agents-auditor**: Audits all custom subagent definitions in a target project for quality, tool restrictions, model selection, and best practices. ([damianwajser/claude-doctor](https://github.com/damianwajser/claude-doctor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agents-auditor.md))

- **agents-baseline**: - Source date: 2026-09-24 - Baseline SHA: `b0500b9a7ecbe37b454f3d917881154c5f7a403c` - Scope: native Agent panel, prompt/context assembly, provider/key lifecycle, tool permissions, SQL/patch confirmations, runtime event flow, provider boundaries, and roadmap consistency. Source claims refer to this SHA. - No source code, tests, plans/status, or runtime state were changed or exercised for this rese ([2tsoftwareapp/db-pro](https://github.com/2tsoftwareapp/db-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/design/agents/agents-baseline.md) · ★1)
- **agents-block**: This folder is a **Lua script for Audiosurf**, run inside the game by the Audiosurf Tweaker plugin. It is a `twllink` project: modules in `src/`, entry point `src/main.lua`, manifest `twproject.json`. `twllink` links them into one `build/&lt;id&gt;.lua` (plus `.lua.map`), and that one file is what the game loads. ([Unchpokable/Audiosurf-Tweaker](https://github.com/Unchpokable/Audiosurf-Tweaker/blob/HEAD/Tools/AgentKit/agents/agents-block.md) · ★6)
- **AGENTS-block**: - Never record, change or remove a pr-rla verdict. Do not run `pr-rla record`, `register`, `freeze`, `sample`, `init`, `checkpoint --push`, `plugin add`, `plugin lock` or `plugin approve`; do not pass `--accept-lock-grants` or `--submit`; do not set `PR_RLA_ACCEPT_LOCK_GRANTS` or `PR_RLA_APPROVALS_FILE`; and do not sign with `ssh-keygen -Y sign -n pr-rla-verdict@v1`. - Never apply or remove a pr-r ([naren-ambati/pr-rla](https://github.com/naren-ambati/pr-rla/blob/HEAD/integrations/agents/AGENTS-block.md))
- **agents-builder**: Use this agent to create a single agent following framework templates and conventions. Invoke when building individual agent components as part of plugin development. ([vanman2024/domain-plugin-builder](https://github.com/vanman2024/domain-plugin-builder/blob/HEAD/plugins/domain-plugin-builder/agents/agents-builder.md) · ★3)
- **AGENTS-CATALOG**: &gt; **Versão:** 2.0 &gt; **Data:** 2026-01-23 &gt; **Total de Agentes:** 28 &gt; **Localização:** `.claude/agents/` &gt; **Integração:** request-optimizer v4.0, Unified Architecture ([matheusallvarenga/claude-code-skills](https://github.com/matheusallvarenga/claude-code-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENTS-CATALOG.md) · ★5)
- **agents-catalog-maintainer**: This document describes the former **agents-catalog-maintainer** custom agent. ([blecx/softwareFactoryVscode](https://github.com/blecx/softwareFactoryVscode/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/agents-catalog-maintainer.md))
- **AGENTS-checklist**: Quick reference for all coding conventions. See linked sub-guides for details. ([IAlphaOmegaI/ecentic](https://github.com/IAlphaOmegaI/ecentic/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/AGENTS-checklist.md))
- **AGENTS-claude**: You are a Spec-Driven Development (SDD) engineer. Intent is committed before code. Documents are always current. Quality gates are non-negotiable. ([kimcharli/ck-skills](https://github.com/kimcharli/ck-skills/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/AGENTS-claude.md))
- **agents-code-style**: - Every agent lives in its own file: `agents/&lt;name&gt;_agent.py`. - Every agent exposes a single `run() -&gt; str` entry point that returns a YAML finding string. - The agentic loop must be bounded by a max turns constant from `agents/config.py` — never an unbounded `while True`. - Partial fallback YAML (`status: partial`) must be returned — never raise or return `None` when the turn budget is exhausted ([vikasparth/restaurant-main](https://github.com/vikasparth/restaurant-main/blob/HEAD/.claude/rules/agents/agents-code-style.md))
- **agents-coded-rules**: Judgment rules for **coded** agents (Python — `main.py` + framework config). Each rule requires the agent to read source and reason — what a regex/AST-emulation/file-walk cannot decide reliably. Same row schema as elsewhere — see [`../rule-format.md`](../rule-format.md). ([UiPath/skills](https://github.com/UiPath/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/uipath-review/references/agents/agents-coded-rules.md) · ★184 · 15 copies)

- **agents-concept**: The agents feature is Attune's **Universal Agent Factory** — one interface for creating, running, and orchestrating AI agents, backed by your choice of framework (native, LangChain, LangGraph, AutoGen, or Haystack) without rewriting code when you switch frameworks. The entry point is **`AgentFactory`**: it picks a framework adapter, and its `create_agent` / `create_workflow` methods return `BaseAg ([Smart-AI-Memory/attune-ai](https://github.com/Smart-AI-Memory/attune-ai/blob/HEAD/.help/templates/agents/concept.md) · ★11)
- **agents-config**: 本页是智能体配置参考，说明页面上的字段如何进入一次运行。新增智能体后端的代码结构见[开发智能体后端](./agent-backend-development.md)。只使用现成智能体时，从[快速开始](../intro/quick-start.md)开始。 ([xerrors/Yuxi](https://github.com/xerrors/Yuxi/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/agents-config.md) · ★7.7k)
- **agents-config**: ARS 的智能体系统基于 LangGraph 构建。配置链路由三个部分组成： ([Zhongye1/KnowAgenticRAG](https://github.com/Zhongye1/KnowAgenticRAG/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/agents-config.md) · ★127)
- **agents-context**: Each agent file uses YAML front matter with `name`, `description`, and `model` fields. The `description` field is what the harness matches against to auto-invoke the agent — it doubles as the trigger condition and must be precise. Every agent explicitly uses `gpt-5.6-terra`. ([vmasrani/dotfiles](https://github.com/vmasrani/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/codex/agents/agents-context.md) · ★4)
- **agents-context**: Each agent file uses YAML front matter with `name`, `description`, and `model` fields. The `description` field is what the Claude harness matches against to auto-invoke the agent — it doubles as the trigger condition and must be precise. Agents that should run on opus say so explicitly in front matter; the rest default to sonnet. ([vmasrani/dotfiles](https://github.com/vmasrani/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/maintained_global_claude/agents/agents-context.md) · ★4)
- **AGENTS-convex**: This codebase uses a structured two-layer architecture for all Convex backend code. The `apps/provider` package is the Convex deployment. Follow these patterns precisely. ([IAlphaOmegaI/ecentic](https://github.com/IAlphaOmegaI/ecentic/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/AGENTS-convex.md))
- **AGENTS-copilot**: You are an expert SDD (Spec-Driven Development) engineer. You prioritize architectural integrity, documented intent, and verified quality over speed. Every change you make is traceable, lint-clean, and consistent with the project's living specifications. ([kimcharli/ck-skills](https://github.com/kimcharli/ck-skills/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/AGENTS-copilot.md))

- **agents-error**: - **The run methods are async.** `invoke`, `run`, and `stream` are coroutines — forgetting to `await` is the most common mistake. - **Non-native frameworks are optional.** They load lazily; check `is_available()` / `list_frameworks(installed_only=True)` before selecting one. - **Scope.** This feature is the Factory; the release agent team and its state store live under release-prep, not here. ([Smart-AI-Memory/attune-ai](https://github.com/Smart-AI-Memory/attune-ai/blob/HEAD/.help/templates/agents/error.md) · ★11)

- **agents-global**: - NixOS with flakes. If a program is missing, run it through `nix-shell -p &lt;package&gt; --run '&lt;command&gt;'` rather than installing it or giving up. - Never `git commit`, `git push`, amend, or force-push without an explicit instruction. Stage the changes, summarize them, then wait. - Stage and commit in separate Bash calls. First `git add` exactly the files for the commit. Then run `git commit -m ...` ([ohaukeboe/dot-emacs](https://github.com/ohaukeboe/dot-emacs/blob/HEAD/workstation/agents/agents-global.md))

- **agents-guide**: Interactive guide for building custom Claude Code Agents and subagents. Asks straightforward questions, generates enhanced YAML frontmatter with tools/model/color/field/expertise, creates agent .md files, validates format, and helps install to .claude/agents/ or ~/.claude/agents/. Use when user wants to build workflow specialist agents. ([alirezarezvani/claude-code-skill-factory](https://github.com/alirezarezvani/claude-code-skill-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agents-guide.md) · ★906 · 7 copies)
